## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 搭档。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can open a task to fix a failing test in one of your repos, and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들면 자주 쓰는 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아서 고쳐 드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 편히 쉬어요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 좋은 밤 보내요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを保証する」通信で、UDPは「速さを優先して、保証はしない」通信です。

TCPは届かなかったデータを再送し、順番も揃えて渡してくれます。UDPは再送も順序保証もなく、送りっぱなしです。そのぶん遅延が小さく、動画のライブ配信やオンラインゲームなどでよく使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día o limpiar una rama tuya que todavía no has compartido, porque deja el historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya está publicada o la usan otros, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠传输：保证数据按序、不丢不重地到达（靠确认和重传），代价是慢一点；UDP 不保证可靠，只管尽快发出去，快但可能丢包、乱序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits before merging. Merge when others have already pulled the branch, since rebasing rewrites history they depend on.
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我先换登录表单的输入框，做完告诉你，再接短信验证码。
  [opened a task] title: 登录页：换成新的输入框组件 | group: 登录页重做 | place: {"kind":"repo","device":"local","repoPath":"~/dev/ducter"} | Ducter gave it to: claude-code (opus) | brief: 用户原话："~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。" ⏎  ⏎ 本任务是第一件：把登录表单换成新的输入框组件。 ⏎  ⏎ 完成标准： ⏎ - 登录表单里的输入框全部改用新的输入框组件，旧的输入框用法不再留在登录页。 ⏎ - 类型检查和现有测试通过，并把命令输出作为证据汇报。 ⏎ - 登录页渲染正常（如能在本地启动，说明看到了什么）。 ⏎ - 改动提交在自己的分支上。 ⏎  ⏎ 不做的事： ⏎ - 不接短信验证码，那是下一件。 ⏎ - 不补端到端测试，那是再下一件。 ⏎ - 不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。 ⏎ - 不改其他页面的输入框，除非新组件的导出必须跟着改。 ⏎  ⏎ 地点：~/dev/ducter 仓库。

  [held in line by Ducter, opens when the part before it is done] title: 登录页：补端到端测试 | group: 登录页重做

### many-asks-places-en

```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段文字的测试文件：

```
bun test foo
```

如果想只跑某个文件里的某几个用例，用 `-t` 按用例名筛选：

```
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it's about to make, before touching the actual data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so no committed change is lost and half-finished ones can be cleaned up.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR 把锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

决定：沿用这个任务已有的分支和草稿 PR，在原分支上提交，不新开分支。

完成标准：
- 按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把锁的范围缩小（只锁真正需要保护的数据），改动限定在 review 指出的地方。
- 跑一遍相关测试并报告结果（命令和输出）；如果有并发相关的测试，说明它是否覆盖了改动的部分。
- 报告里列出改了哪些文件、每处锁的范围从什么变成了什么，并说明没有改动的 review 意见（如果有）。

不在范围内：
- 不合并，不推送到主分支，不把 PR 标记为 ready for review。推送到已有的草稿 PR 分支可以，但先在报告里告诉我，由我再问用户。
- 不改 review 之外的功能逻辑。
```

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的票价都在上面，600 以下的两班，553 起。
  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁周五票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2532 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海，低于 <span id="lim">600</span> 元</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">最便宜 —</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有数据</div>
  <details class="k-add"><summary>改价格线</summary>
    <div class="k-field">
      <label for="limit">低于多少元</label>
      <input class="k-input k-num" id="limit" inputmode="decimal" value="600">
    </div>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var seed = ducter.get("seed") || [];
  var limitEl = document.getElementById("limit");

  function getTrains() { return ducter.get("trains") || seed; }
  function getLimit() { var n = Number(ducter.get("limit")); return n > 0 ? n : 600; }

  function render() {
    var lim = getLimit();
    var trains = getTrains().slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
    var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < lim; });

    document.getElementById("lim").textContent = lim;
    limitEl.value = lim;
    var cnt = document.getElementById("cnt");
    var sub = document.getElementBy … Content = "最便宜 ¥" + under[0].price + "（" + under[0].no + "）";
    } else {
      cnt.textContent = "没有";
      cnt.classList.add("k-none");
      sub.textContent = "低于 ¥" + lim + " 的暂时没有";
    }

    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    document.getElementById("empty").hidden = trains.length > 0;
    trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.textContent = t.no;
      var subEl = document.createElement("div");
      subEl.className = "k-row-sub";
      subEl.textContent = t.note || "";
      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = "¥" + t.price;
      if (t.price < lim) val.style.color = "var(--d-good)";
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(subEl);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  limitEl.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var n = Number(limitEl.value);
    if (n > 0) { ducter.set("limit", n); render(); }
  });

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is set up with the two nights I have so far, 182k writes/s (Night 3) and 181k (Night 2). New runs will add themselves each morning.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1952 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night writes/s</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">No runs yet</div>
  </section>
  <section class="k-card">
    <h2 class="k-h">Runs</h2>
    <div class="k-list" id="runs"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty" style="display:none">The nightly write benchmark results will list here.</div>
  </section>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var runsEl = document.getElementById('runs');
  var emptyEl = document.getElementById('empty');
  var latestEl = document.getElementById('latest');
  var latestSub = document.getElementById('latest-sub');

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];
    });
  }

  function render(runs) {
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    if (!runs.length) {
      runsEl.innerHTML = '';
      emptyEl.style.display = '';
      latestEl.textContent = '—';
      latestSub.textContent = 'No runs yet';
      return;
    }
    emptyEl.style.display = 'none';
    var last = runs[0];
    latestEl.textContent = last.writesPerSec;
    latestSub.textContent = last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
    runsEl.innerHTML = runs.map(function (r) {
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(r.night) +
        '</div><div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + esc(r.p99) + ' · stalls ' + esc(r.stalls) +
        '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + esc(r.writesPerSec) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  var initial = ducter.get("initial") || [];

  var stored = ducter.get('runs');
  if (!stored) {
    ducter.set('runs', initial);
    stored = initial;
  }
  render(stored);
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('runs')); });
  ducter.summary('Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms');
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 3 · Nov 21","writesPerSec":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 2 · Nov 20","writesPerSec":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0}],"initial":[{"night":"Night 3 · Nov 21","writesPerSec":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 2 · Nov 20","writesPerSec":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0}]}

  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>

<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记。记下第一笔就会显示在这里。</div>
</div>
</main>
<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  const total = document.getElementById("total");
  const count = document.getElementById("count");
  const empty = document.getElementById("empty");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  let sum = 0;
  for (const x of xs) {
    sum += Number(x.yuan) || 0;
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  empty.hidden = xs.length > 0;
  if (xs.length) {
    total.textContent = "¥" + sum.toLocaleString("en-US");
    count.textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
  } else {
    total.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
    count.textContent = "还没有花销";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + sum.toLocaleString("en-US") : "还没记");
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, tick each one off as it lands. The count on top shows how many are in.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3089 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Landed</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">0 of 4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tap a PR once it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <p class="k-from" style="color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px">Tap a row to tick it off. Ticks are kept on every device.</p>
</main>
<script>
var items = ducter.get("items") || [];
var landed = {};

function paint() {
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  var n = 0;
  items.forEach(function (it) {
    var done = !!landed[it.id];
    if (done) n++;
    var row = document.createElement("button");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", done ? "true" : "false");
    row.style.cssText = "width:100%;text-align:left;background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;cursor:pointer;color:var(--d-ink);font:inherit";
    var mark = document.createElement("span");
    mark.textContent = done ? "✓" : "";
    mark.style.cssText = "width:22px;height: … Text = "display:block;font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted);margin-top:2px";
    s.textContent = it.repo + " · " + it.status;
    text.appendChild(t);
    text.appendChild(s);
    row.appendChild(mark);
    row.appendChild(text);
    row.addEventListener("click", function () { toggle(it.id); });
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = n + " of " + items.length;
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = n === items.length ? "All landed" : "Tap a PR once it lands";
  if (typeof ducter !== "undefined" && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(n + " of " + items.length + " landed");
}

function toggle(id) {
  landed[id] = !landed[id];
  if (!landed[id]) delete landed[id];
  if (typeof ducter !== "undefined") ducter.set("landed", landed);
  paint();
}

function load(v) {
  landed = (v && typeof v === "object") ? v : {};
  paint();
}

if (typeof ducter !== "undefined") {
  var got = ducter.get("landed");
  if (got && typeof got.then === "function") got.then(load); else load(got);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    var v = ducter.get("landed");
    if (v && typeof v.then === "function") v.then(load); else load(v);
  });
} else {
  paint();
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #412","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","repo":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","status":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","repo":"wey-gu/ducter #418","status":"Draft"}]}

  $("addbox").scrollIntoView({behavior: "smooth", block: "nearest"});

$("save").onclick = () => {
  const amt = $("amt").value.trim();
  if (!amt) return;
  const xs = ducter.get("items") || [];
  const item = {trip: $("trip").value.trim(), what: $("what").value.trim(), day: $("day").value.trim(), cat: cat, amt: amt};
  if (editing >= 0) xs[editing] = item; else xs.push(item);
  ducter.set("items", xs);
  clearForm();
  draw();
};
$("del").onclick = () => {
  const xs = ducter.get("items") || [];
  if (editing < 0) return;
  xs.splice(editing, 1);
  ducter.set("items", xs);
  clearForm();
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

### direct-stable-fact-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: Bun 是用什么语言写的？
  [partner message] Bun 主要用 **Zig** 写的，运行时、打包器、包管理器的核心都是它。底层的 JavaScript 引擎用的是 WebKit 的 **JavaScriptCore**，那部分是 C++，Bun 通过 C++ 绑定来调用它。一些内置模块则直接用 JavaScript 或 TypeScript 写。
```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个米白色、3D 质感的笔记本电脑图标，深蓝色边框，屏幕是空白的，背景透明。

适合放在空状态里。它柔和、友好，不会让人觉得冷冰冰或出错了，屏幕空白也正好对应"还没有内容"。

有几点要注意：
- 它的米白色在纯白背景上对比偏弱，要是页面底色也是浅色，可以稍微加点底色或阴影。
- 图只有 320×246，放大显示会发虚。空状态里一般显示在 120 到 160 像素宽比较合适，要是要大图，最好换一张高清版。
- 屏幕是空的，本身不会告诉用户"这里是什么"，所以旁边最好配一句说明，比如"还没有记录，点这里添加第一条"。
```

### reply-older-task-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 ~/dev/foo 里 TTL 那个时好时坏的测试
(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让它再补一条正好到期那一刻的测试。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

请在现有的假时钟测试基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖 TTL 正好到期那一刻：
- 时钟推进到恰好等于过期时间时，值应当已经视为过期（或按现有约定的边界行为，保持一致并在测试名里写清楚）。
- 时钟比过期时间少一毫秒（或最小单位）时，值仍然有效。
- 只用假时钟，不使用真实 sleep 或真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过，全部 TTL 相关测试连续跑 20 次都通过，把输出结果汇报给我。
范围外：不要合并到主分支，不要推送，不要开 PR。改动留在现有分支上提交即可。
```
